[composebox] Refactor dropdown Tab-exit unselection logic

Moves the logic for unselecting the active dropdown match when pressing
Tab past the last visible match out of `ComposeboxEmbedderMixin` and
into `ComposeboxDropdownElement` and `ComposeboxMatchElement`:

1. Add `omnibox::kComposeboxUnselectMatchOnTabExit` (disabled by
default) in `composebox_features` and expose
`composeboxUnselectMatchOnTabExit` via
`SearchboxHandler::GetWebUIDataSourceDict`.

2. Add `ComposeboxMatchElement.willTabExitMatch()` to encapsulate
whether pressing forward Tab will move focus outside the match rather
than to an internal control (such as the remove button).

3. Add `ComposeboxDropdownElement.unselectOnTabExit()` to check whether
the active match is the last visible match and will exit on Tab,
allowing `getMaxVisibleIndex_()` to stay `protected`.

4. Gate `unselectOnTabExit()` behind `this.unselectMatchOnTabExit` in
`ComposeboxEmbedderMixin.handleTab_()`, falling back to the existing
inline logic when the flag is disabled.

5. Add unit tests in `searchbox_handler_unittest.cc`,
`composebox_autocomplete_test.ts`, `composebox_dropdown_test.ts`, and
`composebox_match_test.ts`.

This is a pure refactoring with no intended behavior change.

Update guest state on new RFH in WebViewGuest::RenderFrameHostChanged

When a speculative RenderFrameHost is created for an unattached
<webview> (such as during an initial newwindow navigation deferred by
CreateWindowThrottle in MPArch), RenderFrameCreated() for that
speculative RenderFrameHost runs before WillAttachToEmbedder() and
DidAttachToEmbedder() configure the guest's view_instance_id, content
scripts, frame name, and transparency on GetGuestMainFrame().

Ensure RenderFrameHostChanged() pushes the current
WebViewRendererState and applies the frame name and transparency to
new_host when it swaps in.

TAG=agy
CONV=d8ac1d3b-b6ed-444b-8f2e-27b4a4ae916e

parent
83d732bd10f1b11a957779d2cdf44a55d4efd559 [ diff ] 
[EVP] Harden Desktop popup dismissal and toast lifecycle

Fix pre-existing lifecycle and dismissal edge cases in Desktop
`EmailVerificationPopupController` and `EmailVerificationController`:

- Resolve pending decision callbacks with `kOther` in `Dismiss()` and
  guard `DidGetUserInteraction()` when `!view_ || is_loading_` so
  dismissing a showing popup never drops its callback or leaves a stale
  callback armed.
- Bind the `ShowPopup()` permission decision wrapper as a non-member
  lambda that checks `WeakPtr` liveness before mutating controller state
  and forwards `callback` unconditionally, so destroying
  `EmailVerificationController` while a popup is showing still resolves
  `callback` via `~EmailVerificationPopupController()`.
- Reject overlapping `Show()` calls while in the loading state with
  `kOverlappingPrompt`.
- Check `WebContents` visibility before showing completion toasts or
  restoring focus on popup dismissal, and dismiss any active popup in
  `ShowLoadingToast()`.

[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in chrome/browser/ [23/25]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs into CHECKs.
The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M161.

See also:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tzGQwGG0lJ6K7Ur8wBW6E58CvJc1JQNyiX0ryHe7A5I/edit?usp=sharing

This patch is generated by the following script:
https://crrev.com/c/7849874/18/tools/memory/dcheck/replace_dchecks_with_checks.py

PSA:
https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/chromium-dev/c/vd1Qt-egELY/m/badCjDnsDAAJ
LSC (Google internal):
http://goto.google.com/chrome-lsc-dcheck-to-check-conversions

97b05e2c2fc4acd60a776ef5b77d74c0d90dc5ca [ diff ] 
Always open downloaded PDFs in Chrome tab

When OpenDownloadInPreferredApp is enabled, opening a downloaded PDF
from Download Manager or the download progress message triggers the OS
intent picker / default app handler, which duplicates the "Open with"
menu action.

To match Desktop Chrome behavior and avoid redundancy with "Open with",
always open downloaded PDFs inline in a new Chrome tab.

Reland "[PA] Replace largest_active_bucket_index_ with active_bucket_count_"

This reverts commit 7722d5682e81c10e435d72e0b4a6ed1fc67853a9.

[CodeHealth] Migrate `PlaybackRateShifter` to `planar_channel_cast()`

Replace the deprecated `AudioBuffer::channel_data()` accessor and raw
pointer casts in `PlaybackRateShifter::FillFrames()` with modern
`AudioBuffer::planar_channel_cast<float>()`.

To supply the legacy `float* const*` parameter of `AudioProvider::
FillFrames()` without heap allocation on the real-time audio thread,
collect channel pointers into a fixed-size stack array bounded by
`kMaxChannels`.

312576a043008642591214196eb909ec4c78f722 [ diff ] 
Lock down //chrome/browser/ui:ui against regaining sources

Move `sources = []` to the very end of //chrome/browser/ui:ui with a
comment stating that no sources may be added here, mimicking
//chrome/browser:browser. Because GN evaluates assignments top to
bottom, a trailing `sources = []` resets any accidental `sources +=`
placed above it back to empty so the target cannot silently regain
sources.

  an empty static_library fails the Windows librarian (lld-link /lib)
  with "lib failed".

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
net: Defer proxied StreamSocket destruction in TransportClientSocketPool
📋 Metadata
commit
7dddd41a3f94cdd49beb6c8a53977d04c91368d2
[ log ]
author
Kenichi Ishibashi <bashi@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 07 05:35:20 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 05:35:20 2026
tree
7e62da84a2ce7149aa022b4665c4452d13740b20
parent
c61d08b3bdc6ac48b08ce000f0ef002a91c6d9e5 [ diff ] 
net: Defer proxied StreamSocket destruction in TransportClientSocketPool

Destroying a tunneled StreamSocket (e.g., a SpdyProxyClientSocket) can
synchronously drain the underlying SpdySession and fail pending
ConnectJobs in the pool. This reentrantly invokes
TransportClientSocketPool::OnConnectJobComplete(), which can modify or
remove Group entries in `group_map_` while the pool is mid-operation.

To avoid reentrancy when closing sockets in a proxied pool, add
`QueueDestroySocket()`, which queues closed `StreamSocket`s into
`sockets_queued_for_destruction_` and posts a task to destroy them
asynchronously when `!GetProxyChain().is_direct()`. Direct connections
continue to be destroyed synchronously.

Bug: 568826881
Change-Id: Ied5765d08ea45050d35f4c746aa84a3c5f66e1ca

parent
f4d4bc4e61ad58c04bd94bdcb13a1258acb1e13c [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Record initial status on SW retries

ServiceWorkerTaskQueue retries service worker registration and start
when they fail with a transient status code. Because the existing retry
histograms only record a boolean outcome, they do not show which status
codes actually recover when retried.

Replace them with the patterned histogram
"ServiceWorkerBackground.{Operation}Retry.{Outcome}.InitialStatus",
which records the status code that triggered the retry sequence, split
by whether the sequence ultimately succeeded or failed. Comparing a
status code's bucket across the two outcomes gives its retry success
rate.

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Extensions.ServiceWorkerBackground.StartWorkerRetryAttemptsResult]=Replaced by Extensions.ServiceWorkerBackground.StartWorkerRetry.{Outcome}.InitialStatus

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAM[Extensions.ServiceWorkerBackground.WorkerRegistrationRetryAttemptsResult]=Replaced by Extensions.ServiceWorkerBackground.RegistrationRetry.{Outcome}.InitialStatus

[Android] Disable letterbox education popup in instrumentation tests

When an app is letterboxed for the first time on Android T+ (e.g. a
test requests portrait orientation on a landscape tablet or desktop),
the system shows a letterbox education popup in its own window. The
popup takes input meant for the app, so a following Espresso click is
lost.

ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest#testMessageResizedOnTablet hit this
on android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel and android-desktop-x64-rel
after it started clicking the tab switcher button through Espresso.

Turn it off with `wm set-letterbox-style --isEducationEnabled false`
during device setup, next to the other system modals the runner
already disables.

Bug: 428024864

[arc] Remove the expired Arc.NearbyShare histograms

All six Arc.NearbyShare.* histograms recorded through
arc_nearby_share_uma.{h,cc} expired on 2025-03-28, so the code that
records them is dead. tools/metrics/histograms/README.md: "Once a
histogram has expired, the code that records it becomes dead code and
should be removed from the codebase."

Remove the helper file and its calls in the bridge, the session, the
share info file handler and the file stream adapter, together with the
streaming start time that only fed the duration histogram. Also remove
the histogram entries and the three enums that only they used. No
behavior change.

R=hidehiko@chromium.org

Bug: None

29ca9542ff98756ee4248cd00816c821c2cb9b13 [ diff ] 
Revert "[🌺] Revert "[SharedPrefsMigration] Clean up part 2""

This reverts commit f95d374931253ef6cbf1afa1cd6dcdd47e25983a.

Reason for revert: Fixed failing tests on android-desktop-x64-rel. Tests failed because resetForTesting() set sData = null, leaving the in-memory store uninitialized for subsequent tests sharing the same process once the SharedPreferences fallback was removed. We fixed this by resetting sData to MultiInstanceData.getDefaultInstance() instead of null, which provides a completely clean slate (0 instances) while preventing assert sData != null crashes.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/try/android-desktop-x64-rel/787460

Original change's description:
> [🌺] Revert "[SharedPrefsMigration] Clean up part 2"
>
> This reverts commit ae2a7c2c07521aad903e4594721620b790899904.
>
> Reason for revert: consistently fails android-desktop-x64-rel.
>
> ContextMenuManagerUnitTest#testShouldShowItem_MultiWindow and LaunchInte
> ntDispatcherTest#testDispatchGlicExternalTrigger_WithConversationId_Rout
> esToTabbedActivity has failed in 3/25 recent builds of android-
> desktop-x64-rel, every attempt in every one of them. This is not flake:
> it reproduces on every run, so it blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL
> that touches this suite. The regression appears at
> refs/heads/main@{#1712049}.
>
> Example failing run:
> https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/try/android-desktop-x64-rel/787460
>
> Replacing the SharedPreferencesManager fallback in
> ChromeMultiInstancePersistentStore.readAllInstanceIds() with `assert
> sData != null` (line 43) causes a deterministic java.lang.AssertionError
> at ChromeMultiInstancePersistentStore.java:43 ->
> MultiWindowUtils.getPersistedInstanceIds(MultiWindowUtils.java:1313) in
> ContextMenuManagerUnitTest#testShouldShowItem_MultiWindow and LaunchInte
> ntDispatcherTest#testDispatchGlicExternalTrigger_WithConversationId_Rout
> esToTabbedActivity because those Robolectric unit tests do not call
> MultiWindowTestUtils.resetInstanceInfo() to initialize sData before
> invoking MultiWindowUtils.
>
> Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
> understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
> not about the merit of the change.
>
> Original change's description:
> > [SharedPrefsMigration] Clean up part 2
> >
> > Fixed: 566026041
> > Change-Id: I6f297e5bc111308003845fbfaba471a804f7529c
> > Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8497006

Reland "Migrate base::Reversed to std::views::reverse"

This is a reland of commit 08815e67f05ca8d96002048cd4bb5ad4e65c92b7

The original change broke the linux-cast-x64-rel build:
overlay_strategy_underlay_cast.cc passed a QuadList to
std::views::reverse, but QuadList does not model
std::bidirectional_range yet. The reland leaves that file on
base::Reversed; it is migrated together with the ListContainer fix in
https://crrev.com/c/8500689.

[OriginGating] Don't expose cache_ member in OriginGatingChecker API

The cache is mostly an implementation detail that need not be exposed to
callers except via metrics values. This CL removes the cache from the
OriginGatingChecker's public API, and adjusts tests to verify the
behavior of the cache, rather than its internal state.

This doesn't change any behavior in production.

Prefer leaving check_includes_strict unset by default.

This allows GN to decide whether check_includes_strict is appropriate
for a given target for you.

For example, if a target has no headers, we should probably assume that
any public_deps were explicitly intended to be exported.

This is part of the check_includes_strict LSC (https://docs.google.com/document/d/1zYUC_zuFrZozo9ogFHcf0Rzkiw9W7RVnaY-1fzfQ0AM/edit?usp=sharing)

Fix CHECK_EQ in CachedMetadataSender::Create for unbound writer
📋 Metadata
commit
01c4bf3467db6ca2e1bd6f55acc46ade42d003a9
[ log ]
author
Eriko Kurimoto <elkurin@google.com>
Wed Oct 07 04:27:56 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 04:27:56 2026
tree
bd6b8ded87135d225f2a4e9045f3bbbb2d88c985
parent
f7a797f50855429cdcf4b267560d736f7409dfbb [ diff ] 
Fix CHECK_EQ in CachedMetadataSender::Create for unbound writer

A kCacheStorage response can have an unbound CacheStorageSideDataWriter
when the response was cloned (e.g., Response.clone() in a Service Worker
or URLResponseHead::Clone() in a throttle) or when the CacheStorage
backend is closed.

Move the CHECK_EQ inside the `is_bound()` branch so we only assert that
a bound writer comes from kCacheStorage, and call DumpWithoutCrashing()
when a kCacheStorage response has an unbound writer while falling
through to NullCachedMetadataSender.

TAG=agy
CONV=3e31b894-38b8-4c65-9032-a5358d54d340

Bug: 548989584, 570132114

[OptimizationGuide] Add ManifestAssetManager state transition tests

Add unit test coverage for ManifestAssetManager state transitions and
registration callbacks:
- Verify CanCreateSession() is deferred while registration is in flight
  and resolves directly to available (when already downloaded) or
  kPendingAssets (when not yet downloaded) once registration completes.
- Verify GetBrokerAssets() reports expected BrokerAssetState values
  across registering, not installed, foreground installing, ready, and
  uninstalling states.
- Verify active ModelClients are invalidated immediately when the user
  opts out of on-device AI before the delayed UninstallComponent() task
  runs.

Wed Oct 07 04:27:24 2026
tree
8d1a86b64a01d026a6d4b3d8f26e5b2dec624391
parent
59acba6a99d42807886e27609fea33ccd91f3285 [ diff ] 
ime: Decrement num_handling_key_events_ when ProcessKeyEvent callback is dropped

This is a follow up of crrev.com/c/8491491.

If a ProcessKeyEvent callback is dropped without being invoked (for
example, in InputMethodEngine::ProcessKeyEvent when the input context
changes while a key event is in flight), ProcessKeyEventDone is never
called. With the in-flight counter introduced in https://crrev.com/c/8491491,
a dropped callback leaves num_handling_key_events_ permanently positive,
causing subsequent non-key-event commits and composition updates to stay
buffered.

Bind a base::ScopedClosureRunner to the ProcessKeyEvent callback so that
num_handling_key_events_ is always decremented whether the callback is
invoked or destroyed without being run.

[iOS] Count Gemini Live prompts in session metrics

Record prompt submissions and increment the session prompt count when
Gemini Live transitions to thinking state. This ensures Live prompts are
included in IOS.Gemini.Session.PromptCount and prevents Live sessions
from being erroneously recorded as abandoned sessions.

parent
f201594de4bc2a60f17655e6d34ed5a5092ddfe0 [ diff ] 
[ttc] Enable the Android-supported tools in ToolController

ToolController's whole tool surface is behind !IS_ANDROID, so an Android
session declares no tools and answers every tool call with kToolUnknown,
even though most of the backing actor tools already build and register
on Android.

Narrow the guard to the four tools whose actor backends are still
excluded from Android builds (skip_android_unmigrated_actor_files):
close_current_tab, switch_tab, open_known_page and set_fullscreen. The
rest are now declared and dispatched on both platforms.

Enabling the tab-based tools on Android also needs a platform-neutral
way to find the session's active tab: the old helper went through
ProfileBrowserCollection and BrowserWindowInterface::GetTabStripModel(),
which has no equivalent on Clank. SessionControllerImpl already tracks
this tab on both platforms via VoiceFocusedContentsTracker (it's where
page context is read from), so expose it as GetVoiceFocusedWebContents()
and use that from PerformActionOnTrackedContents(). set_fullscreen now
takes its window from that tab too, which removes GetActiveBrowser() and
its TODO. The only desktop-visible difference is that a missing window
now reports kTabWentAway rather than kWindowWentAway; nothing
distinguished the two.

Covered by the existing tool_controller_browsertest on desktop, plus a
unit test for the no-tracked-tab error path that runs on both
platforms. Android coverage with a live tab is tracked in
crbug.com/570587982.

Bug: 561651267

parent
a41ace780257ee0c5b192b2a9cb5bd8b0ffb2a77 [ diff ] 
[remoting host] Catch NoSuchProcess and AccessDenied in display_manager_is_gdm

During system boot, transient processes can terminate or become
inaccessible between psutil.process_iter() yielding a process and
inspecting process.name(). When this occurs, psutil raises
NoSuchProcess (or AccessDenied), crashing the Chrome Remote Desktop
host service on startup.

Wrap process inspection in display_manager_is_gdm with try/except
matching the existing pattern in _terminate_kwin_wayland and
_cleanup_stray_processes.

Do not create missing copy source in DownloadFileWithCopy

DownloadFileWithCopy::RenameAndUniquify opens `file_path_to_copy_` only
to read its byte length, but used FLAG_OPEN_ALWAYS | FLAG_READ (which
creates an empty file when the source is absent) and called
file.GetLength() without checking file.IsValid().

Open the copy source with FLAG_OPEN | FLAG_READ and report -1 when the
open fails, which the progress code already treats as an unknown size.

TAG=agy
CONV=19f6c3ce-e464-4999-9bda-f64156a331b6

tree
76ec4f6ff939ee05cf835fa947e2118ecd00f491
parent
e25e1de8b7c44d4eb98d8e67ed355aac15075e22 [ diff ] 
Map File::error_details() on Android FileStream open failure

In net::FileStream::Context::OpenFileImpl, open failures were always
mapped from logging::GetLastSystemErrorCode(). On Android, opening a
Content URI or SAF virtual document path runs through JNI and
ContentResolver rather than POSIX open(), leaving thread-local errno
untouched (and mapping errno == 0 to net::OK on a failed open).

Map base::File::error_details() via FileErrorToNetError() for Content
URIs and virtual document paths so the returned error reflects the
failed File open while preserving OS error mapping on native paths.

TAG=agy
CONV=0386f8b7-ce79-41b6-bed0-2d1111824170

[ttc] Add a chrome://flags entry for Ttc and expose it to Java

TTC's keyed service already compiles into Chrome for Android, but the
only way to turn it on is --enable-features=Ttc on the command line.
Add a chrome://flags#ttc entry on desktop and Android, keyed on the
existing ttc::kTtc feature so a single feature (and a Finch study per
platform) controls TTC everywhere, and expose kTtc through
ChromeFeatureList so the upcoming Android entry point can check it.

No behavior change with the flag off. Enabling it on Android does
nothing yet: nothing there calls TtcKeyedService::StartSession() until
a later CL adds the entry point.

[Vertical Tabs] Limit expand-on-hover to desktop and sync resize handle

1. Only offer expand-on-hover on desktop devices with a precision
   pointer.
2. Show or hide the rail resize handle as soon as expand-on-hover is
   turned on or off. Before, the handle only updated on the next hover,
   because turning expand-on-hover on or off did not request a Side UI
   update when the effective rail state stayed the same.

[Audio] Allow audio::OutputDevice destruction on the UI thread
📋 Metadata
commit
c01dec9e952cae1b99122d28781b00990cc39963
[ log ]
author
James Cook <jamescook@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 07 03:48:30 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 03:48:30 2026
tree
e556cfed54803de563584e4f4af33ebba3cd45ac
parent
1a5d42fa87876d64f173ced94b27df5c32c36cef [ diff ] 
[Audio] Allow audio::OutputDevice destruction on the UI thread

Destroying an audio::OutputDevice joins its realtime audio thread. When
the device is destroyed on a thread that disallows //base sync
primitives, such as the browser UI thread, this hits a DCHECK in
AssertBaseSyncPrimitivesAllowed(). TTC hits this when a
session ends after audio playback has started, because
ttc::AudioController owns its OutputDevice on the UI thread.

Allow the join in CleanUp(), scoped to just audio_thread_.reset().
CleanUp() runs from both ~OutputDevice() and OnConnectionError(), so
the latter no longer needs its own allowance. The join is quick
because AudioDeviceThread shuts down its socket first, which unblocks
the thread. This matches media::AudioInputDevice::Stop(), and
audio::OutputDevice is already a friend of
ScopedAllowBaseSyncPrimitivesOutsideBlockingScope.

Fixed: 570694470
Test: services_unittests --gtest_filter=AudioServiceOutputDeviceTest.*

Android: Replace View / Activity mocks with real objects (simple-3)

Mocking framework classes like View and Activity is discouraged, and
is disallowed by an Error Prone check. This replaces such mocks
in tests with real instances (e.g. new View(), new FrameLayout(),
Robolectric.buildActivity()) and removes the associated
@SuppressWarnings("DoNotMock").

[PiP] Unify PiP eligibility checks on Android

PictureInPicture#isEnabled and
FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController#canDoPictureInPicture
previously performed overlapping eligibility checks separately:
PictureInPicture#isEnabled checked the Android SDK version,
FEATURE_PICTURE_IN_PICTURE, and AppOpsManager.OPSTR_PICTURE_IN_PICTURE,
while FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController checked
FEATURE_PICTURE_IN_PICTURE and BrowserUiUtils#isAndroidAutoProjected
inline without checking AppOpsManager.

Move the BrowserUiUtils#isAndroidAutoProjected check into
PictureInPicture#isEnabled so all device, display, and system-setting
PiP checks share a single source of truth:
- Update ActivityTabWebContentsDelegateAndroid#isPictureInPictureEnabled
  to pass mActivity directly to PictureInPicture#isEnabled.
- Update FullscreenVideoPictureInPictureController#canDoPictureInPicture
  to delegate to PictureInPicture#isEnabled(mActivity).

tree
7f1493ae1f63458a9c35fc99b305d40fba86f9db
parent
0af2c54dcc1d0bc9f8436429800d963cd4736d04 [ diff ] 
[PA] Split PartitionRoot entry points after their prologue

Move what several PartitionRoot entry points do after their prologue
into functions of their own, and likewise how the scheduler-loop
quarantine releases a purged slot. No behavior change.

This prepares for event hooks (https://crrev.com/c/8302832), which
fire an event between an entry point's prologue and the rest, and then
call the rest, as a function, unless a hook took the operation over:
- FreeAfterProlog(): what FreeInlineInternal() does after FreeProlog():
  looks up the object's slot and frees it. FreeInlineInUnknownRoot()
  now calls FreeAfterProlog() directly, rather than
  FreeInlineInternal<kNoHooks>(), whose second FreeProlog() did
  nothing.
- ReallocInPlaceOrMove(): what ReallocInline() does once the request
  is neither an allocation, a free nor too large: resizes the object
  in place if it can, and moves it otherwise. The allocation it moves
  the object to is AllocForRealloc(), which event hooks also hand to
  the early reallocation hook.
- GetExternalUsableSizeFromMetadata(): what GetExternalUsableSize()
  does for a non-null pointer.
- ZapBrpQuarantinedObject(): what QuarantineForBrp() does without a
  QuarantineOverrideHook.
- ReleaseDequarantinedSlot(): how the scheduler-loop quarantine
  releases a purged slot, into the thread cache or batched in its
  BatchFreeQueue. The purge now takes the entry off its list before
  releasing it, rather than after, so that a release that re-enters
  the purge cannot find the entry again.

53da5f888bf8f2134be948472cf4a1495124b07f [ diff ] 
[PA] Replace FreeFlags::kWith*Hint with typed FreeHint arguments

Replace FreeFlags::kWithSizeHint, FreeFlags::kWithAlignmentHint, and
FreeFlags::kWithTypeIdHint (and the FreeHintType<...> struct) with
strongly-typed hint arguments (FreeSizeHint, FreeAlignmentHint,
FreeTypeIdHint) constrained by the FreeHint concept.

This unifies the hinted and unhinted overloads of PartitionRoot's
Free*() entry points into a single variadic template:
  template <FreeFlags flags = FreeFlags::kNone, FreeHint... Hint>
and moves the alignment-adjusted size calculation into
FreeNoHooksImmediate() so that FreeInlineInUnknownRoot() can call
FreeInlineInternal<kNoHooks>() directly instead of the out-of-line
FreeInline<kNoHooks>() when a hint is provided.

No behavior change.

Android: Replace View / Activity mocks with real objects (simple-2)

Mocking framework classes like View and Activity is discouraged, and
is disallowed by an Error Prone check. This replaces such mocks
in tests with real instances (e.g. new View(), new FrameLayout(),
Robolectric.buildActivity()) and removes the associated
@SuppressWarnings("DoNotMock").

2ae8281029fa3238451f6ff00700a84647269db8

author

Wed Oct 07 03:29:57 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 03:29:57 2026
tree
920450f0e05670c0eb3fec4f1def4f04f082d59b
parent
a1a5fb4b6e62d7bb36ced70c2bcaefb42b140606 [ diff ] 
Remove allow_circular_includes_from from //third_party/protobuf:protoc_lib

This previously existed because :protoc_lib was a static_library
containing core compiler sources/headers while also depending on the
language generator source_sets (:protoc_cpp, :protoc_java, etc.), which
in turn included headers from :protoc_lib. Additionally,
compiler/cpp/enum.h was mistakenly listed in protoc_headers instead of
protoc_cpp_headers.

To remove it, we move compiler/cpp/enum.h to protoc_cpp_headers and
extract :protoc_lib_sources from :protoc_lib so the language generator
targets can depend on :protoc_lib_sources while :protoc_lib aggregates
them.

Part of LSC: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1LuFqCJwtzZHuLfoj9yuupZnlCqwnbDXBgmF70tCwxDE/edit?tab=t.0

InitialWebUI is enabled on Linux.

configures the window, so verify only the restored window size on those
platforms.

committer

Wed Oct 07 03:29:23 2026
tree
30444e2b4815183d7f382bca1aada331ac5f79c8
parent
7d3c3af749a72d5a0973ca2dd6f1fa67e6de1c05 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Add TabHandle <-> ActorSurfaceHandle conversions

Adds ActorSurfaceHandle::From(tabs::TabHandle) and the inverse
ActorSurfaceHandle::GetTabHandle(). Both resolve through the live
surface (matching on its tab backing) rather than reusing the raw id,
so callers keep working once surface and tab ids diverge. Missing
surfaces or tabs map to the respective Null() handle.

This lets the in-flight migrations (task, tools, input plumbing)
convert between tab and surface handles at their boundaries without
assuming the two share raw values.

[pip-ext] 7. Guard null BrowserWindowInterface in TabsHighlightFunction

In TabsHighlightFunction::Run(), check that
window_controller->GetBrowserWindowInterface() is non-null before
dereferencing it in ExtensionTabUtil::GetEditableTabList(). For
standalone window controllers without a BrowserWindowInterface (such as
Document Picture-in-Picture windows), return kTabStripNotEditableError
instead of crashing on a null pointer dereference.

allowlisted to be copied in the node_modules archive in
crrev.com/c/8404106. Unfortunately a recent roll of a package
(Stylelint 17.0.0->17.15.0 in crrev.com/c/8398137) created a
node_modules archive before the allowlisting change landed, and
did not re-create the archive after re-basing.

Original change's description:
> [Linux] Add portal-based system proxy resolver
>
> Implement proxy_resolver::mojom::SystemProxyResolver on Linux using the
> org.freedesktop.portal.ProxyResolver XDG desktop portal, and pass it to
> the network service when --use-system-proxy-resolver is passed. The
> portal applies the host's proxy configuration (including PAC scripts
> and WPAD) on the host side, which allows proxy settings to work in
> sandboxed environments such as Flatpak without granting access to dconf
> or kioslaverc.
>
> ChromeMojoProxyResolverLinux runs on the UI thread and uses the shared
> D-Bus session bus. The portal's availability is determined from the
> ProxyResolver interface version. Proxy URIs returned by the portal
> (direct://, http://, https://, socks://, socks4://, socks5://) are
> converted to ProxyChains; credentials are dropped, since ProxyServer
> doesn't support them.
>
> Design doc: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1-v2xvo068vv77l6JqLjCdFgFVh0JES8JSuIErlJyWrI/edit?usp=sharing&resourcekey=0-jNAMNOXydV4qtji-33ZY2w

[Glic] Show keyboard shortcut settings only with hardware keyboard

The "Turn on keyboard shortcut" toggle and the "Keyboard shortcut
[Alt] [G]" row in Gemini in Chrome settings previously only checked
whether local state prefs were available, so they showed up on phones
without a physical keyboard.

Gate the launcher toggle and hotkey rows on
KeyboardUtils.isHardKeyboardConnected(context) (while keeping the
navigation shortcut row additionally gated on
GlicUtils.isSidePanelFormFactor(context)), and remove the shortcut rows
from the settings search index when no hardware keyboard is connected.

Bug: 568044219

Use a null ItemAnimator during the NTP feed scroll restore
📋 Metadata
commit
937cd34dea774690aa9982504f90eaecdf6a5d62
[ log ]
author
Raghu Rai <raghurai@google.com>
Wed Oct 07 02:46:35 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 02:46:35 2026
tree
741015d9fcc1b2ce25863a42220883db044d7b7c
parent
d93996fe06b06894b6cf650598e2ab1a980ee543 [ diff ] 
Use a null ItemAnimator during the NTP feed scroll restore

When navigating back to the NTP, FeedSurfaceMediator briefly installs
ItemAnimatorWithoutAnimation after restoring the scroll position. It
returned false from every animate*() method without calling
dispatchAnimationFinished(). RecyclerView marks each ViewHolder it hands
to the animator as not recyclable and only releases it on
dispatchAnimationFinished(), so any item laid out during that window
stayed busy forever. If such an item was later removed while on screen
(e.g. the load-more spinner, or cards replaced by a refresh), its view
stayed attached as a hidden child: still drawn behind the cards, but no
longer laid out or scrolled. This leads to an unreferenced spinner
animation which does not go away (as seen in: b:517926260).

Fix: Set the ItemAnimator to null for the restore window instead.
RecyclerView then skips item animations entirely and never hands
ViewHolders to an animator, so none of them can get stuck. The original
animator is restored once the restore completes, as before.

Feature flag added: FeedNullItemAnimatorOnScrollRestore; enabled by
default. Disabling it restores the previous behaviour.

The bug is hard to reproduce: ItemAnimatorWithoutAnimation is only
installed for about one frame (3-7 ms measured on a Pixel 9), and a list
change (e.g. a refresh result or the load-more spinner) has to land
inside that frame. It was recreated artificially on an emulator with a
local debug build that kept the animator installed for 15s after the
scroll restore. A pull-to-refresh inside that window left the removed
items behind as stale views, while the same build with this fix left
none.

Recording of the artificially reproduced bug (UX intentionally modified
for the red background and bottom margin):
http://b/477909353#attachment82743683

Bug: b:517926260, b:477909353, b:442734590

Show scheduled restart bubble as inactive to preserve Omnibox focus
📋 Metadata
commit
d93996fe06b06894b6cf650598e2ab1a980ee543
[ log ]
author
Anunoy Ghosh <anunoy@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 07 02:46:06 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 02:46:06 2026
tree
0fdc4bd8adc0db1af51a047a6c1f84a4b6440f14
parent
eeee1ecec1e7653a220b47c881716defb083551e [ diff ] 
Show scheduled restart bubble as inactive to preserve Omnibox focus

When the Scheduled Restart nudge appears on a newly opened New Tab
Page, showing the bubble as active steals keyboard focus away from the
Omnibox and places it on the default "Relaunch now" button. If the user
is typing a search query or URL, their keystrokes are interrupted and
pressing Space or Enter can unintentionally relaunch the browser.

Display the bubble as inactive (matching RelaunchRecommendedBubbleView)
so the Omnibox retains focus, and set the standard inactive bubble
accessibility description so screen readers announce how to focus the
dialog.

TAG=agy

Bug: 546850768

Revert "[remoting] Add file_util for flat deployment and staged in-use rename"

This reverts commit 24baa63021740954a65c52f4d982c06c60af6f47.

Reason for revert: Test failure.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/win11-arm64-rel-tests/8969/overview

Fixed: 570811427

Original change's description:
> [remoting] Add file_util for flat deployment and staged in-use rename
>
> Introduces file deployment and uninstallation utilities for the Windows
> native host installer under //remoting/host/installer/win/setup:
>
> - Shared Payload Definition (host_payload_files.gni & payload_files.h):
>   Factors the canonical list of Windows host binaries, manifests, and
>   resources into //remoting/host/installer/win/host_payload_files.gni.
>   This list is shared between remoting_me2me_host_archive and a GN
>   header generator in setup/BUILD.gn that produces payload_files.h
>   (kPayloadFiles), preventing build-to-runtime drift.
> - In-Use File Updates (InstallFileWithStagedRename):
>   Supports updating running host executables and loaded DLLs without
>   terminating active remote sessions. If a destination file is locked,
>   the existing binary is renamed to <filename>.old
>   (or <filename>.<N>.old across consecutive updates while earlier
>   binaries remain mapped) and scheduled for deletion on the next reboot
>   before copying the new binary into the canonical path.
> - Flat Directory Deployment & Uninstallation (DeployPayloadFiles,
>   UninstallPayloadFiles, CleanupOldFiles):
>   Deploys payload files into the flat installation directory, cleans up
>   unlocked *.old files left over from prior updates, and removes
>   installed files and the installation directory on uninstall.
>
> TAG=agy
> CONV=73cc74e2-2635-48e7-b08e-6e7645d7bdeb

[Linux] Add portal-based system proxy resolver

Implement proxy_resolver::mojom::SystemProxyResolver on Linux using the
org.freedesktop.portal.ProxyResolver XDG desktop portal, and pass it to
the network service when --use-system-proxy-resolver is passed. The
portal applies the host's proxy configuration (including PAC scripts
and WPAD) on the host side, which allows proxy settings to work in
sandboxed environments such as Flatpak without granting access to dconf
or kioslaverc.

ChromeMojoProxyResolverLinux runs on the UI thread and uses the shared
D-Bus session bus. The portal's availability is determined from the
ProxyResolver interface version. Proxy URIs returned by the portal
(direct://, http://, https://, socks://, socks4://, socks5://) are
converted to ProxyChains; credentials are dropped, since ProxyServer
doesn't support them.

Design doc: https://docs.google.com/document/d/1-v2xvo068vv77l6JqLjCdFgFVh0JES8JSuIErlJyWrI/edit?usp=sharing&resourcekey=0-jNAMNOXydV4qtji-33ZY2w

R=mmenke@chromium.org

[svg] style reuse (3/3): Handle mutations and enable

Route changes to existing SVG presentation-attribute values through
kIndependentStyleChange when the corresponding CSS property supports
incremental styling. Property additions, removals, unsupported changes,
and updates that require rebuilding the presentation-attribute style
continue to request a full local style recalc.

Handle both attribute mutations and SVG DOM baseVal updates. Notify
selector invalidation for baseVal changes, since these bypass normal
attribute notifications and can no longer rely on a full recalc to
rematch selectors.

This change also enables SvgIncrementalStyle by default. The performance
gains are substantial in dynamic SVG content:

Speedometer3: +0.2% overall score

Motionmark: +2.1% overall score

blink_perf.svg SvgNestedUse: +26%

Android: Replace View / Activity mocks with real objects (medium-1)

Mocking framework classes like View and Activity is discouraged, and
is disallowed by an Error Prone check. This replaces such mocks
in tests with real instances (e.g. new View(), new FrameLayout(),
Robolectric.buildActivity()) and removes the associated
@SuppressWarnings("DoNotMock").

[iOS] Deflake BookmarksFolderChooser move and undo EGTests

In `util_testMoveAndUndoFromModel:toModel:`, tapping "Undo" on the
snackbar triggers `[BookmarksHomeViewController refreshContents]` and
`[UITableView reloadData]`, which marks cells as `hidden` until the next
`-[UITableView layoutSubviews]` pass completes. Calling the non-waiting
`assertWithMatcher:grey_sufficientlyVisible()` immediately after Undo
races with that layout pass on `ios-fieldtrial-rel` (where
`--disable-field-trial-config` disables `UseSceneViewController`,
`ChromeNextIa`, and `PageActionMenu`).

Use `waitForSufficientlyVisibleElementWithMatcher:` for `Folder 2` after
tapping Undo, consistent with the check before tapping Undo, and remove
the gardening suppressions from `ios/testing/test_expectations.txt`.

TAG=agy
CONV=47a915f7-5e7d-4f73-b3e1-e05258948bf2

[loomnibox] Add Dynamic Sizing and Small Font options

Adds default-disabled feature parameters and chrome://flags options for
Omnibox Everywhere:
- "Enabled with Small Font (14px)" (SmallFont/true): applies 14px font
  sizing while keeping the default 480px width.
- "Enabled with Dynamic Sizing (680px)" and "(600px)":
  rests at 480px (5 MVT tiles) in persistent mode and expands from the
  horizontal center to 680px (7 tiles) or 600px (6 tiles) when active.

Fix flaky renderer kill in AutofillContextMenuManager browser tests

This CL fixes flakiness in
PasswordsFallbackWithUIInteractionsTest#SuggestPasswordTriggersPasswordGenerationAndRecordsMetrics

In BaseAutofillContextMenuManagerTest::CreateAndAttachPasswordForm(),
the test previously set the form URL to "https://myform.com/" and called
content::OverrideLastCommittedOrigin(main_rfh(), ...) after navigating
to an embedded_test_server() HTTP page.

Because OverrideLastCommittedOrigin() mutates the browser-side
last_committed_origin_ while the renderer document remains at the
embedded_test_server() origin (http://127.0.0.1:<port>), an in-flight
ManifestManagerHost::GetManifest() request triggered during page load
can receive a default manifest whose start_url is populated from the
renderer's document URL after the browser's last_committed_origin_ was
overridden. ManifestManagerHost::OnRequestManifestResponse() then fails
the same-origin check in MaybeGetBadMessageStringForManifest() and
terminates the renderer process with a bad Mojo message ("Manifest
start_url ... must be same-origin with the document"), causing flaky
failures in SuggestPasswordTriggersPasswordGenerationAndRecordsMetrics.

Fix this by using main_rfh()->GetLastCommittedURL() for the attached
password form instead of overriding the main frame's committed origin.

test history here shows flakiness -
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium%3Aci/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3APasswordsFallbackWithUIInteractionsTest%23SuggestPasswordTriggersPasswordGenerationAndRecordsMetrics

[Remoting] Fix cross-sequence WeakPtr race in RemotingRendererFactory
📋 Metadata
commit
f058e0f29fc5ef6eab1598c8e7081537fb081b18
[ log ]
author
Jordan Bayles <jophba@chromium.org>
Wed Oct 07 02:19:24 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Wed Oct 07 02:19:24 2026
tree
4ef80cf8bf3711f9b40852cca3140fafc4263a17
parent
9723f12f7df3a13f6314ea05025016d8e07cde72 [ diff ] 
[Remoting] Fix cross-sequence WeakPtr race in RemotingRendererFactory

RemotingRendererFactory is constructed, operated (CreateRenderer),
and destroyed on the renderer main thread, but previously bound its
weak_factory_ to media_task_runner_ via BindPostTask for
OnAcquireRenderer, and passed base::Unretained(this) for
OnAcquireRendererDone (which Receiver::SetRemoteHandle invoked on
the media sequence). This caused a cross-sequence WeakPtr race and UAF
when RemotingRendererFactory was destroyed on the main sequence while
callbacks were in flight on the media sequence, as well as cross-thread
RpcMessenger access.

Fix this by:
1. Running OnReceivedRpc and OnAcquireRenderer directly on
   main_task_runner_, where RpcMessenger dispatches messages and
   where RemotingRendererFactory lives.
2. Tracking whether a Receiver is waiting for a remote handle via a
   boolean flag (has_waiting_receiver_) so checking on the main
   sequence does not invoke WeakPtr::operator bool() (which would bind
   Receiver's WeakPtrFactory to the main sequence instead of the media
   sequence).
3. Wrapping OnAcquireRendererDone with
   base::BindPostTask(main_task_runner_, ...) bound to
   weak_factory_.GetWeakPtr() so it safely executes on the main
   sequence.
4. Migrating manual thread checks to SEQUENCE_CHECKER in
   RemotingRendererFactory and ReceiverController.

Bug: 500600456
Fixed: 500600456
Test: media_remoting_unittests

[🌺] Disable 1 consistently failing test

ChromeAndroidTaskIntegrationTest#createPendingTask_requestSetBoundsAndDeactivate_dispatchesBoth
failed all retry attempts (hard-failed the build) in 3/25 (12%) recent
builds on android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests (#4571, #4575, #4577) and
flaked in 4/25 (16%) additional builds (#4554, #4555, #4560, #4572),
impacting 7/25 (28%) of recent builds.

Disabled here:
  ChromeAndroidTaskIntegrationTest.createPendingTask_requestSetBoundsAndDeactivate_dispatchesBoth (desktop only)

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/ci/android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests/4577

Why disable rather than revert:
Fails with java.lang.AssertionError: This Task is not alive during
ChromeActivity.onDestroy on Android 17 Desktop (#4571, #4575, #4577;
also flaked in #4554, #4555, #4560, #4572); no single recent commit in
the range introduced this regression.

This is mitigation, not a fix. The bug tracks the real investigation and
is assigned to the owning team.

The class-level @DisableFeatures(GLIC_BACKGROUND_ACTUATION) was added as

feature landed. The underlying test breakage has since been fixed, and
the suite passes with the feature in its default (fieldtrial-enabled)
state, so remove the override and the TODO.

parent
1f1d2d0cb25ccf6351fc53c3246ae80fbaae2bdd [ diff ] 
[ROSP] Default session startup pref to LAST on Android desktop

Default the session startup preference to restore the last session on
Android desktop when kSyncRestoreOnStartupPref is enabled, aligning the
default pref value with the existing startup window policy behavior.

This was previously pending a privacy review because this update changes
session cookie restoration behavior. Now that we have privacy approval
to align this behavior on Android desktop with other desktop platforms,
we can update the default preference in native code instead of adding
checks at the Java callsite.

* Return LAST from SessionStartupPref::GetDefaultStartupType() on
  Android desktop when kSyncRestoreOnStartupPref is enabled.
* Remove the temporary default preference value workaround in
  TabbedStartupWindowPolicyDelegate now that the native default matches.

tree
6dddaaa826ba050e62e76f600f5e7aee53e8b28e
parent
5ac1b072e85d6035cd81685ccd1574480a8e9381 [ diff ] 
[ReadAloud] Compute the estimated total playback duration

Add PlaybackTimeline::GetTotalDuration(), which returns the estimated
1.0x duration of the whole document: the per-character estimate of
every chunk plus the deviations recorded by UpdateSentenceDuration()
so far. The total converges to the actual duration as chunks are
synthesized.

ResolveTimeOffset() already computed this sum privately to clamp
targets past the end of the document. It now reuses the new method.

Nothing calls GetTotalDuration() outside tests yet. A follow-up CL uses
it to report playback progress and duration to the browser, which
today drops every progress update because the real controller never
reports a duration.

[PWA] Replace single-window PWA title with app name

Previously, standalone PWAs displayed the window title in the format
"App Name - Page Title". When only a single window of a standalone PWA
is open, this change replaces the window title with just "App Name". If
multiple windows of the same PWA are open, "App Name - Page Title" is
retained to help users distinguish between windows.

This is a reland of https://crrev.com/c/6619606, which was reverted in
https://crrev.com/c/6659184. The original change broke ChromeOS Tast
tests for the Terminal System Web App, which uses a tab strip and relies
on the active tab title being present in the top-level window title.
This reland excludes ChromeOS System Web Apps and tabbed web apps from
single-window title simplification.

50557978e0f9d1982f1f3ce4973afed3e72fa3a6 [ diff ] 
[Code Health] Remove Activity mock in ChromeContextMenuPopulatorTest

Replace @Mock private Activity mActivity with a real Activity instance
in ChromeContextMenuPopulatorTest.

On 32-bit x86 Android 10 emulator builders (android-10-x86-rel),
ByteBuddy's AndroidClassLoadingStrategy runs out of memory during DEX
bytecode translation when attempting to mock android.app.Activity,
causing MockitoException OutOfMemoryError and cascading failures across
all test methods in the batch shard.

mActivity is only used to register ActivityState.CREATED with
ApplicationStatus.onStateChangeForTesting, so a real Activity instance
avoids dynamic proxy DEX generation entirely and removes the need for
@SuppressWarnings("DoNotMock").

ElementTiming: "Commit" ElementTiming entries at paint time

This CL unifies emitting text and image element timing entries and
ensures we emit entries based on paint-time criteria, as opposed to
filtering at presentation, which we were doing for text but not images.
This changes things to capture the navigation ID at paint time, rather
than at presentation time.

This is a step towards creating all paint timing entries at paint time.
separating this part off and using ElementTimingInfo allows us to
decouple both splitting off ContainerTiming into its own client and
migrating LCP/ICP to committing to entries at paint time. In the next
patch, we'll do the LCP/ICP migration and change OnPaintFinished() to
return a callback, which for ElementTiming, will own/manage the list of
ElementTimingInfos (removing it from PaintTiming).

Reland "[Tab UI] Convert TabUiTestHelper#enterTabSwitcher() to hop on Public Transit"

This is a reland of commit a17f49c0a20027255d94c38bc707bb5f7d3a8815

Reverted because ArchivedTabsDialogCoordinatorTest#testMessageResizedOnTablet
failed consistently on android-desktop-x64-rel: the tab switcher button
click did not open the HUB. The test rotated to portrait right before
entering the tab switcher, which letterboxes Chrome on landscape
desktops, and Android's first-time letterbox education popup swallowed
the Espresso click. The old performClick() bypassed input dispatch.

crrev.com/c/8520058 restricted that test to tablets, since desktop
can't rotate to portrait. The popup also shows on large tablets
(android-14-tablet-landscape-arm64-rel), so this also adds
TabUiTestHelper#enterTabSwitcherProgrammatically(), which keeps the old
performClick() behavior, with a TODO to remove it, and uses it in that
test.

Original change's description:
> [Tab UI] Convert TabUiTestHelper#enterTabSwitcher() to hop on Public Transit
>
> First conversion of a legacy test util with TrafficControl#hopOnAt().
>
> enterTabSwitcher(), used by 31 test files, now hops on at the current
> page (a CtaPageStation) and travels to the TabSwitcherStation by
> clicking CtaPageStation#tabSwitcherButtonElement, via the existing
> openRegularTabSwitcher()/openIncognitoTabSwitcher(). This replaces
> performClick() on a raw findViewById() plus a layout wait, and resolves
> the crbug.com/40155797 TODO to click the button through Espresso. The
> util now returns the TabSwitcherStation. Public Transit callers can
> continue from that Station; the others ignore it. Hopping on over a live
> Station in the same Activity logs a warning pointing the caller at
> CtaPageStation#openRegularTabSwitcher() instead.
>
> Also adds TrafficControl#hopOnAt(Activity, Station), the shorthand for
> the common case of hopping on without any action, i.e.
> Triggers.noopTo().hopOnTo(), the counterpart of hopOffPublicTransit(),
> and uses it in HopOnOffUnitTest.
>
> tab_ui:test_support_javalib gains direct deps on public_transit_java and
> chrome_java_transit, whose types TabUiTestHelper now uses.
>
> The waitForLayout(BROWSING) after addBlankTabs() in TabGridDialogTest is
> removed: addBlankTabs() already waits for the BROWSING layout, and the
> duplicate wait was dead code.
>
> Change-Id: If6866b265c196cd56c926ef10a107be5d018dbc5
> Reviewed-on: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8503109

8172f39e37523e88ffa4d13d9f1f5f3cd298d00b [ diff ] 
Remove gfx::HdrMetadataExtendedRange in favor of AGTM

This structure was used for several different things. One of them was
plumbing the Android desired_brightness_ratio, which is now removed.

The other was indicating that a buffer should render as HDR (the
"extended" tone mapping mode). AGTM is capable of representing the same
thing, but specifying a baseline HDR headroom and no alternate images.
Add the function gfx::HDRMetadata::SetExtendedRangeWithHeadroom, which
configures the AGTM metadata this way.

There were several consumers of extended_range that have been changed to
check for AGTM. Most of these should be changed to use the
cc::ToneMapUtil functions in the future. They include:
- TextureLayer redraws on headroom change.
- TextureLayerImpl reports HDR content color usage.
- The Mac HDR copier layer is used.

[ChroMenu] Support --app-menu-search-query switch for testing

Adds support for the `--app-menu-search-query` command-line switch in
ActionAppMenu. When specified, ActionAppMenu pre-fills the search bar
with the provided query, queries AppMenuSearchController, and renders
the search results underneath the search bar on menu open.

Individual search result items are normalized to DisplayType::kRow so
that actions originally defined with custom layouts (such as New Tab or
New Window block buttons) render cleanly as standard clickable rows.

To use add --app-menu-search-query="<your search term here>" to the end
of the build command. Clicking the app menu will display the text inside
the search bar.

[TTC] Replace PageContextFetcher with PageContentExtractionService in
TTC PCM

Replace TtcPageContextMonitor's PageContextFetcher-based pull model with
a push model backed by PageContentExtractionService (PCES), and send the
extracted page context to the conversation.

- TtcPageContextMonitor::Observer replaces PageChangedCallback:
  - OnPageContextInvalidated(): the monitored page changed (PCES
    OnPageContentReset()).
  - OnPageContextFetched(result): fresh APC was extracted for the
    monitored page.
- TtcPageContextMonitor observes PCES instead of the WebContents and is
  no longer a WebContentsObserver. Navigation invalidations come from
  PCES OnPageContentReset(), which fires for every cross-document
  navigation and for significant same-document ones.
- On construction (and via FetchPageContext()), content already cached
  by PCES is delivered to observers asynchronously; otherwise an
  extraction is triggered.
- PageContext now shares PCES's ref-counted APC instead of copying it,
  and records the page URL. Eligibility comes from PCES's synchronous
  lookup, which is accurate within OnPageContentExtracted() as of the
  parent CL. PDF text is skipped.
- SessionControllerImpl forwards fetched page context to
  Conversation::SendContextUpdate(). If PCES is unavailable (page
  content annotations disabled), no monitor is created.
- Remove PageContextFetcher, StartNewFetch() and
  SessionController::GetPageContext().
- TtcKeyedServiceFactory now DependsOn PageContentExtractionServiceFactory.

Behavior changes:
- Registering as a PCES observer enables automatic extraction for every
  tab in the profile (PageContentExtractionEnablementReason::
  kObserverRegistered) while a TTC session has a monitored tab.
- APC is extracted with PCES's options rather than
  DefaultAIPageContentOptions(on_critical_path=true) with
  max_meta_elements = 32.
- Every voice-focused tab change triggers an extraction for the new tab
  if PCES has no cached content for it.
- Page changes are no longer signalled when loading stops
  (DidStopLoading()); PCES extracts once the page has settled.

e74f4ea3aa013d5eca839892214e0511638079a0
parent
4f41cc2e7a7c80b0a135ada4e63ed63011719690 [ diff ] 
[Media] Fix CueTimeline bugs on synchronous focusout during cue transfer

Removing a focused cue display box via VTTCue::RemoveDisplayTree()
synchronously dispatches blur/focusout events. If an event listener
re-adds or transfers the cue between tracks:

- TextTrack::addCue() could proceed with cue->track() re-populated by
  the focusout listener, leaving the cue registered in multiple
  tracks/timelines.

- TextTrack::CueWillChange() removed the display tree before updating
  cue timing fields, allowing a focusout listener to re-insert the cue
  into CueTimeline::cue_tree_ under the old interval before
  CueDidChange() inserted the new interval.

- TextTrack::setMode() and CueTimeline::RemoveCues() removed display
  trees before updating mode_ or detaching all cues in the list.

Guard these synchronous script re-entrancy points so stale raw
TextTrackCue* intervals cannot remain in CueTimeline::cue_tree_.

[Composebox]: Gate NTP submission on delayed tab snapshots

https://screencast.googleplex.com/cast/NjE3MjEwOTEwODY3NDU2MHxlYzQxYWQ1ZS01Mw

A delayed tab is only uploaded on submit, from a snapshot the browser
takes after `addTabContext()` returns. The NTP let users submit (or
click a suggestion) before that snapshot existed, so the tab was
silently dropped. Hold submission until `kProcessing` confirms the
snapshot, tolerating it arriving before the `addTabContext()` reply.
While waiting, submit is disabled and voice input is kept but not
submitted. A fetch failure, including the existing 60s timeout, releases
the hold.

Bug: 552185361

d62d20667c445612095e3b542035edf6283e135d [ diff ] 
[XR] Fix XrBrowserTestBase flakes from clock skew and EvalJs crashes

XrBrowserTestBase serves test pages over an HTTPS EmbeddedTestServer
whose leaf certificate chains to the static root_ca_cert.pem, which has
a hardcoded validity period starting Oct 10, 2025. On Android test
devices whose system clock has reset to an earlier date, certificate
verification fails with ERR_CERT_DATE_INVALID, causing navigation in
LoadFileAndAwaitInitialization() to fail.

Because ASSERT_TRUE only returns from the current helper and the helper
did not set javascript_failed_, the test then continued running
JavaScript on about:blank, and EvalJsResult::ExtractBool() hit a
CHECK(is_ok()) and crashed the test process. The resulting CRASH status
had no primary error message, so LUCI Analysis filed per-test flake
bugs instead of clustering on the real failure.

This CL:
- Uses RootType::kUniqueRoot so the root CA validity is relative to the
  device's current time, and trusts it via a ScopedTestRoot.
- Sets javascript_failed_ whenever navigation, initialization, ExecJs,
  or polling fails, and short-circuits subsequent JavaScript helpers.
- Checks EvalJsResult::is_bool()/is_string() before extracting values,
  reporting a test failure instead of crashing.

[TabStrip] Share LTR/RTL test bodies in StripLayoutHelperTest (4/5)

Share test bodies between LTR/RTL test pairs in StripLayoutHelperTest.
-Previously, several LTR and RTL tests were near-identical copies that
 differed mainly in RTL setup and in which side (left/right) they
 asserted on.
-The copies were hard to keep in sync, and one RTL test was not
 actually running in RTL.
-Now, each pair calls a shared private helper that takes an rtl
 parameter, and the existing test names remain as thin wrappers.

Make the RTL desktop close button test actually run in RTL.
-Previously, it called LocalizationUtils#setRtlForTesting(true) and
 then StripLayoutHelperTest#initializeTest, which reset RTL to false.
-Now, the shared helper passes rtl to initializeTest, so the RTL
 variant runs in RTL and still passes.

2bfd4e0f0a6ca86057832d3fcf70ab903520d164 [ diff ] 
[PDF Ink Signatures] Record annotation type metrics on save

Record boolean histograms PDF.Ink2SaveHasStrokes and
PDF.Ink2SaveHasTextAnnotations every time a PDF document is saved with
Ink2 changes in PdfInkModule::RecordMetricsOnSave(). These metrics track
whether the saved PDF contains any brush/highlighter strokes (including
both newly drawn strokes and pre-existing V2 Ink shapes that have not
been erased) and whether it contains any text annotations (including
both newly added and pre-existing text annotations that have not been
deleted).

Because both histograms are recorded on every save with Ink2 changes,
each histogram's total sample count represents the total number of saves
as a denominator.

TAG=agy
CONV=c30e2377-4c4c-46f8-95ff-62ed1796a521

1344d1880002085ee25b2394f1a7cac4c4ffe7d7

9a51a128195f326e3730f2296dbd68881c902580 [ diff ] 
[TabUI Virtualization] Add base data provider and flat snapshot reset

TabListMediator and its layout delegates currently observe TabModel
directly, requiring UI controllers to manually translate global TabModel
indices around non-tab message cards and pre-allocate List<Tab>
snapshots for filtered surfaces (TabGridDialog, TabGroupUi,
TabListEditor).

This is [1/n] of the TabUI data virtualization refactor and introduces
only the base contracts and full-reset snapshot flow (TabListItem,
TabListDataObserver, TabListDataProvider, and FlatTabListDataProvider)
to decouple UI presentation from raw TabModel observation.
FlatTabListDataProvider projects predicate-filtered TabModel state into
immutable TabItem entries and pushes full-reset snapshots via
onDataReset(), attaching to TabModel on requestDataReset() and detaching
on stop() or model switch. Subsequent CLs will add incremental
structural diffs, property payload updates, and TabListMediator
integration.

Bug: 562590772
